                                                   Cinnamon Almond Butter
Ingredients                        Directions
2 cups whole raw almonds            Preheat an oven to 350 degrees F (175 degrees C).
3 tablespoons white sugar
1 tablespoon brown sugar            Spread the almonds into a single layer on a baking sheet.
1 tablespoon ground cinnamon
1/2 teaspoon salt, or more to taste Roast the almonds in the preheated oven until dark brown, about 8
                                    minutes. Be careful to not overcook. Allow to cool completely
                                    before proceeding.

                                   Blend the almonds in a blender until thick and creamy, 8 to 10
                                   minutes. The mixture will be very dry initially and then begin to
                                   clump. Scrape the sides of the blender with a rubber spatula as
                                   needed to keep the almonds blending. Add the white sugar, brown
                                   sugar, cinnamon, and salt; continue blending until completely
                                   smooth. Store in refrigerator.

                                   Return the mixture to the bowl of dry ingredients and stir in the

                                   275 degrees F.

                                   inch wide. Place the slices, cut side down, onto cookie sheets.

                                   Bake 25 to 30 minutes in the preheated oven, until the biscotti is
                                   crispy and lightly toasted.

                                   in the oven. When cool, store in an airtight container.
